     業務回顧及展望
     BUSINESS REVIEW AND OUTLOOK

     業務回顧                          BUSINESS REVIEW

     我們是一家全球領先的現製飲品企業，聚焦為          We are a leading global freshly-made drinks company. We
     廣大消費者提供單價約人民幣6 元（約1 美元）       are committed to providing high-quality value-for-money
     的高質平價的現製果飲、茶飲、冰淇淋、咖啡          products to consumers, including freshly-made fruit drinks,
     和現打鮮啤等產品。我們旗下有現製茶飲品牌          tea drinks, ice cream, coffee and fresh beer, typically priced
     「蜜雪冰城」、現磨咖啡品牌「幸運咖」及現打鮮        around one U.S. dollar (approximately RMB6) per item. We
     啤品牌「鮮啤福鹿家」。以2025年度的終端零售       have three major brands – our freshly-made tea drinks brand,
     額計，我們在全球食品飲料行業排名第62位。         MIXUE, our freshly-made coffee brand, Lucky Cup, and our
                                   fresh beer brand, FULU Fresh Beer. In 2025, we ranked 62nd
                                   in the global food and beverage industry in terms of gross
                                   merchandise value (GMV).


     我們持續打造並強化以「供應鏈+品牌IP+門店        We strengthen our core competitiveness through a holistic
     運營」為核心的競爭力，力爭實現「三位一體的         strategy that drives excellence across supply chain, brand IP
     總成本領先」。首先，積極建設行業領先的端到         and store operations, propelling overall cost leadership. First,
     端供應鏈體系，全面覆蓋採購、生產、物流、          we consistently enhance our best-in-class, end-to-end supply
     研發等環節，同步實施嚴格的質量管控與效率          chain encompassing procurement, production, logistics,
     優化舉措，為消費者提供更高質平價的產品。          R&D and more, underpinned by rigorous quality control and
     其次，着力打造家喻戶曉的全民品牌和深受消          efficiency optimization measures, to offer products with
     費者喜愛的IP，形成獨特的消費者心智佔位。         higher quality and greater value for money. Second, we strive
     與此同時，建立標準化的運營管理體系，通過          to build household brands and endearing IPs to capture
     健康、可持續發展的加盟模式實現規模化運           consumer mindshare. Meanwhile, we implement standardized
     營；並持續提升數字化運營能力，從而為消費          operation management systems, enabling scalable growth
     者提供更優的消費體驗。                   through a healthy and sustainable franchise model. We also
                                   continue to improve our digital operation capabilities to
                                   provide consumers with a better consumption experience.


     2025年，在國內及海外市場，我們持續聚焦供        In 2025, we continued to focus on supply chain enhancement,
     應鏈強化、品牌IP建設和門店運營優化，旨在         brand IP building, and store operation optimization in
     為消費者打造更高質平價的產品和更優的消費          domestic and overseas markets, aiming to create high-quality
     體驗。截至2025年12月31日，我們已在全球       value-for-money products and engaging experiences for
     （涵蓋中國及海外13個國家）構建起約60,000家     our consumers. As of December 31, 2025, we have built a
     的門店網絡。                        network of approximately 60,000 stores worldwide (covering
                                   China and 13 other countries).

2025 年，現製飲品行業發生深刻變化，也帶    In 2025, the freshly-made drinks industry underwent profound
來了新的運營挑戰。年內，第三方線上平台加      changes, bringing new operational challenges. During the
大對現製飲品消費的補貼力度，這一方面為行      year, third-party online platforms increased subsidies for
業帶來更多曝光量，另一方面也推動訂單向線      freshly-made drinks consumption. This not only brought
上遷移，對各品牌數字化運營能力形成直接考      greater exposure to the industry but also accelerated the shift
驗。此外，補貼推高了消費者對現製飲品質價      of orders to online channels, testing brands’ digital operational
比的期待，對各品牌的產品力提出更高要求。      capabilities. Furthermore, subsidy-driven promotions raised
為此，我們持續「修煉內功」，在夯實以「供應     consumers’ expectations for value for money, setting a higher
鏈+品牌IP+門店運營」為核心的競爭力的基礎    bar for product competitiveness across the industry. To
上，重點提升我們的數字化運營能力及產品質      navigate this landscape, we continued to strengthen our core
價比，積極應對行業變化帶來的各類考驗。       capabilities. While further solidifying our core competitiveness
                          built on “Supply Chain + Brand IP + Store Operations,” we
                          also prioritize improving our digital operational capabilities
                          and product value-for-money, proactively addressing the
                          evolving challenges brought by industry transformation.

     我們的加盟模式                       Our Franchise Model


     我們主要採用加盟模式來擴張我們的門店網           We primarily employ a franchise model to grow our store
     絡。在該模式下，我們授權加盟商開設加盟門          network. Under this model, we authorize our franchisees to
     店並使用我們的品牌銷售現製飲品，同時向我          sell freshly-made drinks through franchised stores under
     們購買門店物料及設備用於其日常運營。作為          our brands, while purchasing store supplies and equipment
     加盟門店的擁有者，加盟商對門店的經營業績          from us as part of their daily operations. Franchisees are the
     負責，且須遵循我們全面、標準化的運營流程          owners of these stores and are accountable for their results
     及要求。                          of operations. We also stipulate that franchisees adhere to
                                   our comprehensive, standardized operational procedures and
                                   requirements.


     我們始終踐行「與加盟商利益與共」的理念，通         Under the philosophy of aligning interests with franchisees,
     過一系列制度設計與加盟商共同建立了健康、          we have systematically devised a range of policies and
     可持續的加盟模式。我們為加盟商提供從選           measures to establish a healthy and sustainable franchise
     址、培訓到運營全週期的支持，在助力其持續          model. We empower our franchisees with comprehensive
     成長的同時，也為我們業務的可持續發展奠定          support covering site selection, training and store operations,
     堅實的基礎。從收入模式而言，我們不以加盟          helping them achieve sustained growth while also laying
     費和相關服務費為主要收入。2025年，加盟費        a solid foundation for the sustainable development of our
     及相關服務費收入佔比僅為2.4%。我們大規模        business. In terms of our revenue model, franchise and related
     的極致高效的供應鏈體系為加盟商提供了具有          service fees are not our primary sources of revenue. In 2025,
     競爭力的一站式解決方案，從而增強其市場競          the revenue from franchise and related services accounted
     爭力及盈利能力，亦有效提升消費者體驗。同          for only 2.4% of our total revenue. Our expansive and highly
     時，我們通過高度數字化的運營體系對加盟商          efficient supply chain provides franchisees with a competitive
     進行標準化的管理以及持續不斷的賦能，從而          one-stop solution, improving their competitiveness and
     提升加盟商運營效率。在利益與共的理念下，          profitability while effectively elevating consumer experience.
     我們與加盟商共同實現了行業領先的門店規模。         Meanwhile, we further support our franchisees with
                                   standardized management and a highly-digitalized operational
                                   system to enhance their operational efficiency. By aligning
                                   interests with franchisees, we have together achieved an
                                   unparalleled store scale.

現金管理政策                         CASH MANAGEMENT POLICY

我們相信，通過對短期投資產品進行適當的投           We believe we can make better use of our cash by making
資，我們可以更好地利用我們的現金，在不影           appropriate investments in short-term investment products,
響我們的業務運作或資本開支的情況下產生收           which generate income without interfering with our business
益。我們對金融產品的投資決策均採個案評估           operation or capital expenditures. Our investment decisions
方式，經審慎考量各項相關因素後作出，包括           with respect to financial products are made on a case-by-case
但不限於宏觀經濟形勢、市場狀況、投資產            basis and after careful consideration of a number of factors,
品的預期收益及潛在損失、投資成本、投資期           including, but not limited to, macroeconomic conditions,
限。我們已經建立了一套內部政策與指引，使           market conditions, the expected return and potential loss
我們能夠獲得合理的投資回報，同時監控並管           of the investment, investment cost, and the duration of the
理投資風險。每項投資產品，無論其交易金額           investment. We have established a set of internal policies and
大小，均須經過內部審核及批准程序。該等政           guidelines which allow us to achieve reasonable returns on
策與程序由高級管理層所制定。                 our investment while monitoring and controlling investment
                               risks. Every investment product, regardless of its transaction
                               amount, is required to undergo internal review and approval
                               procedure. These policies and procedures were formulated
                               by our senior management.

     主要業務                      PRINCIPAL BUSINESS

     我們是一家全球領先的現製飲品企業，聚焦為      We are a leading global freshly-made drinks company. We
     廣大消費者提供單價約人民幣6 元（約1 美元）   are committed to providing high-quality value-for-money
     的高質平價的現製果飲、茶飲、冰淇淋、咖啡      products to consumers, including freshly-made fruit drinks,
     和現打鮮啤等產品。我們旗下有現製茶飲品牌      tea drinks, ice cream, coffee and fresh beer, typically priced
     「蜜雪冰城」、現磨咖啡品牌「幸運咖」及現打鮮    around one U.S. dollar (approximately RMB6) per item. We
     啤品牌「鮮啤福鹿家」。以2025年度的終端零售   have three major brands – our freshly-made tea drinks brand,
     額計，我們在全球食品飲料行業排名第62位。     MIXUE, our freshly-made coffee brand, Lucky Cup, and our
                               fresh beer brand, FULU Fresh Beer. In 2025, we ranked 62nd
                               in the global food and beverage industry in terms of gross
                               merchandise value (GMV).

     公眾持股量充足性                       SUFFICIENCY OF PUBLIC FLOAT

     上市規則第8.08條規定，尋求上市的證券必須         Rule 8.08 of the Listing Rules requires there to be an open
     有一個公開市場，且發行人的上市證券須維持           market in the securities for which listing is sought and a
     足夠的公眾持股量。這一般指無論何時，發行           sufficient public float of an issuer’s listed securities to be
     人已發行股本總額必須至少有25%由公眾人士          maintained. This will normally mean that at least 25% of the
     持有。                            issuer’s total issued share capital must at all times be held by
                                    the public.


     本公司已向聯交所申請，而聯交所已授予豁免           The Company has applied to the Stock Exchange for,
     嚴格遵守上市規則第8.08(1)(a)條的規定，將本     and the Stock Exchange has granted, a waiver from strict
     公司最低公眾持股量調減至以下較高者：(a)本         compliance with Rule 8.08(1)(a) of the Listing Rules to reduce
     公司已發行股本總額的10.00%，及(b)緊隨全       the minimum public float of the Company to the higher of
     球發售完成後公眾持有的H股百分比（按因悉數          (a) 10.00% of the total issued share capital of the Company,
     行使超額配股權而將發行的H股而增加）。緊隨          and (b) such percentage of H Shares to be held by the public
     悉數行使超額配股權後，由公眾人士持有的H           immediately after completion of the Global Offering, as
     股數目佔本公司已發行股本總額約10.61%。         increased by the H Shares to be issued upon any exercise of
                                    the Over-allotment Option. Immediately after the full exercise

     主要風險與不確定因素                 Key Risks and Uncertainties


     我們的營運涉及若干主要風險與不確定因素，       Our operations involve a number of key risks and
     其中一部分在我們的控制範圍之外。下文載列       uncertainties, some of which are beyond our control. The
     我們面對的重大風險與不確定因素：           material risks and uncertainties we face are set out below:


     •   我們的業務依賴於消費者對我們產品的需     •    Our business relies on consumer demand for our
         求，而這種需求本質上是不斷變化的，並          products, which is ever-evolving in nature and may
         可能會隨著消費者偏好和觀念以及其可支          shift upon changes in consumer preferences and
         配消費能力的變化而變化。消費者對我們          perceptions, as well as consumers’ discretionary
         產品需求的不利變化將損害加盟商和我們          spending power. Adverse changes in consumer demand
         的業務。                        for our products will harm our franchisees’ and our
                                     business.


     •   我們未來的增長取決於我們能否不斷擴張     •    Our future growth depends on our ability to continuously
         我們門店網絡的覆蓋範圍及成功管理我們          extend the footprint of our store network and
         龐大的門店網絡的運營績效。               successfully manage the operational performance of our
                                     expansive store network.


     •   我們所處的市場競爭激烈，如果我們未能     •    We operate in a highly competitive market and
         有效競爭，則中國現製飲品市場的激烈競          intensifying competition in China’s freshly-made drinks
         爭可能會對我們的市場份額及盈利能力造          market could materially and adversely impact our market
         成重大不利影響。                    share and profitability if we fail to compete effectively.


     •   近期中國現製飲品市場在競爭加劇中放      •    A recent slowdown in China’s freshly-made drinks
         緩，可能對我們的業務及經營業績造成不          market amid intensifying competition may adversely
         利影響。                        impact our business and results of operations.

潛在分拆的狀況                   Status of the Potential Spin-off


如招股章程所披露，考慮到本公司海外業務的      As disclosed in the Prospectus, having considered, among
規模及快速增長，以及其與本公司在中國內地      others, the size and rapid growth of the Company’s overseas
的業務在地理位置及市場側重方面的明確區分      businesses and its clear delineation with the Company’s
等因素，本公司擬保留於上市後三年內分拆海      business in Chinese mainland in terms of geographical
外業務的可能性（「潛在分拆」）。因此，本公司    locations and market focus, the Company wishes to retain
已申請豁免嚴格遵守第15項應用指引第3(b)段   the possibility to spin off its overseas businesses within three
項下有關本集團海外業務潛在分拆的三年限制      years after the Listing (the “Potential Spin-off”). As such,
規定，且聯交所已授出有關豁免。潛在分拆仍      the Company has applied for, and the Stock Exchange has
將受第15項應用指引的其他規定所規限。       granted, a waiver from strict compliance with the three-year
                          restriction requirement under paragraph 3(b) of Practice
                          Note 15 in relation to the Potential Spin-off of the overseas
                          businesses of the Group. The Potential Spin-off will remain
                          subject to other requirements of Practice Note 15.

分拆的任何詳細計劃，包括時間表、上市地點      does not have any detailed plan in relation to the Potential
及將予分拆的實體。本公司將繼續遵守適用規      Spin-off, including the timetable, the listing venue and the

及法規公佈其詳情（如有）。儘管如此，潛在分     applicable rules and regulations, and announce the details of

     企業管治文化與價值觀                  CORPORATE GOVERNANCE CULTURE AND VALUE

     本公司致力確保按照高道德標準開展公司事         The Company is committed to ensuring that its affairs are
     務，堅信在實現長期目標的過程中，必須採         conducted in accordance with high ethical standards. This
     取正直、透明和負責任行動的信念。本公司相        reflects its belief that, in the achievement of its long-term

風險管理及內部控制                 RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS

董事會確認其對風險管理及內部控制系統之責      The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk
任，並負責檢討該等系統之有效性。該等系統      management and internal control systems and for reviewing
旨在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標之風險，      their effectiveness. Such systems are designed to manage
且僅可就重大失實陳述或損失提供合理而非絕      rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business
對之保證。                     objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute
                          assurance against material misstatement or loss.


董事會全權負責評估及釐定本公司為實現戰略      The Board has the overall responsibility for evaluating and
目標而願意承擔之風險性質及程度，並建立及      determining the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to
維持適當且有效之風險管理及內部控制機制。      take in achieving the Company’s strategic objectives, and
                          establishing and maintaining appropriate and effective risk
                          management and internal control mechanisms.


審計委員會協助董事會監督風險管理及內部控      The Audit Committee assists the Board in leading the
制系統之設計、實施及監察。             management and overseeing their design, implementation
                          and monitoring of the risk management and internal control
                          systems.

      董事就綜合財務報表須承擔的責任           Responsibilities of the directors for the consolidated
                                financial statements


      貴公司董事負責根據國際會計準則理事會頒佈      The directors of the Company are responsible for the
      的國際財務報告會計準則及香港公司條例的披      preparation of the consolidated financial statements that
      露要求擬備真實而中肯的綜合財務報表，並落      give a true and fair view in accordance with IFRS Accounting
      實其認為必需的內部控制，以確保綜合財務報      Standards as issued by the IASB and the disclosure
      表的擬備不存在重大錯誤陳述（不論是否由欺      requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance,
      詐或錯誤導致）。                  and for such internal control as the directors determine is
                                necessary to enable the preparation of consolidated financial
                                statements that are free from material misstatement, whether
                                due to fraud or error.


      在擬備綜合財務報表時， 貴公司董事負責評      In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the
      估貴集團持續經營的能力，在適用情況下披露      directors of the Company are responsible for assessing the
      與持續經營有關的事項以及使用持續經營作為      Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, disclosing, as
      會計基準，除非貴公司董事有意將貴集團清盤      applicable, matters related to going concern and using the
      或停止經營，或別無其他實際的替代方案。       going concern basis of accounting unless the directors of
                                the Company either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease
                                operations or have no realistic alternative but to do so.

            號：2097.HK）。本公司的註冊辦事處位                              code: 2097.HK). The registered office of the Company is

      2.   會計政策                      2.   ACCOUNTING POLICIES

           2.1 擬備基準                       2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION


               該等財務報表乃根據國際財務報告                  These financial statements have been

     2.4 重大會計政策                    2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES


       於聯營公司投資                        Investment in associates

       聯營公司是指本集團長期持有其權                 An associate is an entity in which the Group has a
       益股票權一般不少於20%且對其擁                long term interest of generally not less than 20%
       有重大影響力的實體。重大影響力                 of the equity voting rights and over which it has
       是指有權參與被投資公司的財務及                 significant influence. Significant influence is the
       經營政策的決策，但並非控制或共                 power to participate in the financial and operating
       同控制該等政策。                        policy decisions of the investee, but is not control
                                       or joint control over those policies.


       本集團於聯營公司投資乃根據會計                 The Group’s investments in associates are stated
       權益法，按本集團應佔的資產淨值                 in the consolidated statement of financial position
       扣除任何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況                 at the Group’s share of net assets under the equity
       表內呈列。任何可能存在的不同會                 method of accounting, less any impairment losses.
       計準則已作出調整以使其一致。                  Adjustments are made to bring into line any
                                       dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.


       本集團應佔聯營公司收購後業績及                 The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results
       其他全面收入份額分別計入綜合損                 and other comprehensive income of associates
       益表及綜合其他全面收入。此外，                 is included in the consolidated statement of profit
       倘於聯營公司的權益內直接確認一                 or loss and consolidated other comprehensive
       項變動，則本集團會於綜合權益變                 income, respectively. In addition, when there has
       動表內確認其於任何有關變動的應                 been a change recognised directly in the equity of
       佔份額（倘適用）。本集團與聯營公                the associate, the Group recognises its share of
       司交易所產生的未變現盈虧，均按                 any changes, when applicable, in the consolidated
       本集團於聯營公司的投資為限進行                 statement of changes in equity. Unrealised gains
       抵銷，除非未變現虧損證明已轉讓                 and losses resulting from transactions between
       資產出現減值則除外。收購聯營公                 the Group and its associates are eliminated to
       司產生的商譽計入本集團於聯營公                 the extent of the Group’s investments in the
       司的投資的一部分。                       associates, except where unrealised losses
                                       provide evidence of an impairment of the assets
                                       transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition
                                       of associates is included as part of the Group’s
                                       investments in associates.

               業務合併及商譽                         Business combinations and goodwill

               業務合併採用收購法入賬。轉讓代                 Business combinations are accounted for using
               價按收購日期的公允價值計量，該                 the acquisition method. The consideration
               公允價值為本集團轉讓的資產、本                 transferred is measured at the acquisition date fair
               集團向被收購方前擁有人承擔的負                 value which is the sum of the acquisition date fair
               債，以及本集團為換取被收購方控                 values of assets transferred by the Group, liabilities
               制權而發行的股權於收購日期的公                 assumed by the Group to the former owners of
               允價值之總和。對於每一項業務合                 the acquiree and the equity interests issued by
               併，本集團選擇以公允價值或被收                 the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree.
               購方可識別淨資產的比例份額計量                 For each business combination, the Group elects
               被收購方的非控股權益。非控股權                 whether to measure the non-controlling interests
               益的所有其他組成部分均按公允價                 in the acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate
               值計量。收購相關成本於發生時計                 share of the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All
               入當期損益。                          other components of non-controlling interests are
                                               measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs
                                               are expensed as incurred.


               當收購的一組活動及資產包含一項                 The Group determines that it has acquired a
               投入及一項實質性過程，且兩者共                 business when the acquired set of activities and
               同對創造產出的能力有重大貢獻                  assets includes an input and a substantive process
               時，本集團確定其收購了一項業務。                that together significantly contribute to the ability
                                               to create outputs.


               當本集團收購一項業務時，其會根                 When the Group acquires a business, it assesses
               據收購日期的合約條款、經濟環境                 the financial assets and liabilities assumed for
               及相關條件，評估所承接的金融資                 appropriate classification and designation in
               產及負債以進行適當的分類及指定。                accordance with the contractual terms, economic
                                               circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the
                                               acquisition date.


               收購方轉讓的任何或然代價於收購                 Any contingent consideration to be transferred
               日期按公允價值確認。分類為資產                 by the acquirer is recognised at fair value at
               或負債的或然代價按公允價值計                  the acquisition date. Contingent consideration
               量，公允價值變動計入損益。分類                 classified as an asset or liability is measured at fair
               為權益的或然代價不予重新計量，                 value with changes in fair value recognised in profit
               其後續結算於權益內入賬。                    or loss. Contingent consideration that is classified
                                               as equity is not remeasured and subsequent
                                               settlement is accounted for within equity.

       業務合併及商譽（續）                       Business combinations and goodwill (Continued)

       商譽初始按成本計量，成本為轉讓                  Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the
       代價、就非控股權益確認的金額以                  excess of the aggregate of the consideration
       及本集團先前持有的被收購方股權                  transferred, the amount recognised for
       的任何公允價值之總和，超出所收                  non-controlling interests and any fair value of the
       購可識別資產及所承擔負債的部                   Group’s previously held equity interests in the
       分。倘此代價及其他項目之總和低                  acquiree over the identifiable assets acquired and
       於所收購淨資產的公允價值，則其                  liabilities assumed. If the sum of this consideration
       差額在重新評估後，於損益中確認                  and other items is lower than the fair value of
       為議價購買收益。                         the net assets acquired, the difference is, after
                                        reassessment, recognised in profit or loss as a
                                        gain on bargain purchase.


       初始確認後，商譽按成本減任何累                  After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at
       計減值虧損計量。商譽每年進行減                  cost less any accumulated impairment losses.
       值測試，倘有事件或情況變動顯示                  Goodwill is tested for impairment annually or more
       賬面值可能發生減值，則更頻繁地                  frequently if events or changes in circumstances
       進行測試。本集團於每年 12 月 31              indicate that the carrying value may be impaired.
       日對商譽進行年度減值測試。就減                  The Group performs its annual impairment test
       值測試而言，於業務合併中收購的                  of goodwill as at 31 December. For the purpose
       商譽自收購日期起被分配至預期將                  of impairment testing, goodwill acquired in a
       從合併的協同效應中受益的本集團                  business combination is, from the acquisition date,
       各現金產出單元或現金產出單元組                  allocated to each of the Group’s cash-generating
       別，無論本集團的其他資產或負債                  units, or groups of cash-generating units, that
       是否被分配至該等單元或單元組別。                 are expected to benefit from the synergies of the
                                        combination, irrespective of whether other assets

                                        units or groups of units.


       減值通過評估商譽相關的現金產出                  Impairment is determined by assessing the

               公允價值計量                          Fair value measurement

               本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計                 The Group measures its certain financial assets
               量其若干金融資產及負債。公允價                 and liabilities at fair value at the end of each
               值為於計量日期在市場參與者間進                 reporting period. Fair value is the price that would
               行的有序交易中出售資產所收取或                 be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a
               轉移負債所支付的價格。公允價值                 liability in an orderly transaction between market
               計量乃假設出售資產或轉移負債的                 participants at the measurement date. The fair
               交易於資產或負債的主要市場進                  value measurement is based on the presumption
               行，或在未有主要市場的情況下，                 that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer
               則假設於資產或負債的最有利市場                 the liability takes place either in the principal
               進行。主要或最有利市場必須為本                 market for the asset or liability, or in the absence
               集團可進入的市場。資產或負債的                 of a principal market, in the most advantageous
               公允價值乃基於市場參與者為資產                 market for the asset or liability. The principal or the
               或負債定價時所用的假設計量（假設                most advantageous market must be accessible by
               市場參與者以符合彼等最佳經濟利                 the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability
               益的方式行事）。                        is measured using the assumptions that market
                                               participants would use when pricing the asset or
                                               liability, assuming that market participants act in
                                               their economic best interest.

       為產生現金流量管理金融資產的方                 assets refers to how it manages its financial assets
       式。業務模式釐定現金流量會否來                 in order to generate cash flows. The business

       產或來自以上兩者。按攤銷成本分                 from collecting contractual cash flows, selling the
       類及計量的金融資產按持有金融資                 financial assets, or both. Financial assets classified
       產目的為收取合約現金流量的業務                 and measured at amortised cost are held within a
       模式持有，而以公允價值計量且其                 business model with the objective to hold financial
       變動計入其他全面收益分類及計                  assets in order to collect contractual cash flows,
       量的金融資產按目的為持有以收取                 while financial assets classified and measured at
       合約現金流量及出售的業務模式持                 fair value through other comprehensive income are
       有。不屬於上述業務模式持有的金                 held within a business model with the objective of

       入損益分類及計量。                       selling. Financial assets which are not held within
                                       the aforementioned business models are classified
                                       and measured at fair value through profit or loss.

                                       that result from default events that are possible

       時透過實際利率攤銷程序於損益表                   they are stated at cost. Gains and losses are
       確認。                               recognised in the statement of profit or loss when
                                         the liabilities are derecognised as well as through
                                         the effective interest rate amortisation process.


       攤銷成本經計及任何收購折扣或溢                   Amortised cost is calculated by taking into
       價及組成實際利率整體部分的費用                   account any discount or premium on acquisition
       或成本計算得出。實際利率攤銷計                   and fees or costs that are an integral part of the
       入損益表中的財務成本內。                      effective interest rate. The effective interest rate
                                         amortisation is included in finance costs in the
                                         statement of profit or loss.

     判斷（續）                        Judgement (Continued)


     投資物業與自用房地產的分類                Classification between investment properties and
                                  owner-occupied properties

     本集團確定一項物業是否符合投資物業的           The Group determines whether a property qualifies as
     條件，並已制定作出該判斷的標準。投資           an investment property, and has developed criteria in
     物業是指為賺取租金或為資本增值或兩者           making that judgement. An investment property is a
     兼有而持有的物業。因此，本集團會考慮           property held to earn rentals or for capital appreciation
     一項物業是否在很大程度上獨立於本集團           or both. Therefore, the Group considers whether a
     持有的其他資產而產生現金流量。有些物           property generates cash flows largely independently of
     業的一部分是為賺取租金或資本增值而持           the other assets held by the Group. Some properties
     有，另一部分是為用於生產或提供商品或           comprise a portion that is held to earn rentals or for
     服務或為行政管理目的而持有。如果這些           capital appreciation and another portion that is held for
     部分能夠單獨出售或通過融資租賃單獨出           use in the production or supply of goods or services or
     租，本集團將分別對這些部分進行會計處           for administrative purposes. If these portions could be
     理。如果這些部分不能單獨出售，則僅在           sold separately or leased out separately under a finance
     為用於生產或提供商品或服務或為行政管           lease, the Group accounts for the portions separately. If
     理目的而持有的部分不重大的情況下，該           the portions could not be sold separately, the property
     物業才被歸類為投資物業。本集團會根據           is an investment property only if an insignificant portion
     個別物業作出判斷，以確定附帶服務是否           is held for use in the production or supply of goods or
     重大到使該物業不符合投資物業的條件。           services or for administrative purposes. Judgement
                                  is made on an individual property basis to determine
                                  whether ancillary services are so significant that a
                                  property does not qualify as an investment property.

      11. 所得稅                                11. INCOME TAX

          本集團須按實體基準就於本集團成員公司                    The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on
          註冊及經營所在稅務司法管轄區產生或取                    profits arising in or derived from the tax jurisdictions in
          得的利潤繳納所得稅。                            which members of the Group are domiciled and operate.


          中國企業所得稅                               PRC corporate income tax


          根 據《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 企 業 所 得 稅 法 》      Under the Law of the PRC on Enterprise Income Tax
         （「《企業所得稅法》」）及《企業所得稅法實                  (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the
          施條例》，本集團中國附屬公司的企業所                    EIT Law, the EIT rate of the Group’s PRC subsidiaries is
          得稅稅率為25%，惟獲得下文所載稅項豁                   25% unless subject to tax exemption set out below.
          免者除外。


          從事「西部地區鼓勵類產業」業務的若干                    Certain subsidiaries that are engaged in businesses in
          附屬公司可享有15%的企業所得稅優惠稅                   the “Encouraged Industries in the Western Region” and
          率。                                    eligible for the preferential EIT rate of 15%.


          於海南自由貿易港註冊及運營，且符合核                    Certain subsidiaries that are domiciled and operate in
          心業務收入佔60%以上標準的若干附屬公                   Hainan Free Trade Port which meet the criteria of having
          司，可享有15%的企業所得稅優惠稅率。                   more than 60% of the revenue generated from their core
                                                businesses are eligible for the preferential EIT rate of
                                                15%.


          若干附屬公司從事農產品預處理，可免徵                    Certain subsidiaries are engaged in agricultural product
          有關稅項。                                 pre-treatment and eligible for relevant tax exemptions.

          截至2025年及2024年12月31日止年度，               Hong Kong profits tax has been provided at the rate of
          香港利得稅已按於香港產生的估計應課稅                    16.5% on the estimated assessable profits arising in

          屬公司根據利得稅兩級制屬合資格實體除                    2024, except for two subsidiaries of the Group which are
          外。該等附屬公司的首2,000,000港元應                qualifying entities under the two-tiered profits tax rates
          課稅利潤按8.25%的稅率徵稅，而餘下應                  regime. The first HK$2,000,000 of assessable profits of
          課稅利潤則按16.5%的稅率徵稅。                     the subsidiaries are taxed at 8.25% and the remaining

34. 儲備                           34. RESERVES

   本集團的儲備金額及其變動於財務報表的               T h e a m o u n t s o f t h e G r o u p’ s r e s e r v e s a n d t h e

   (a)   法定儲備                       (a)   Statutory reserve


         根據中國公司法，在中國登記的公                  In accordance with the Company Law of the PRC,
         司須將法定稅後利潤的10%分配                  companies registered in the PRC are required to
         至法定儲備，直至該儲備總額達到                  allocate 10% of the statutory after tax profits to the
         公司註冊資本的50%。經中國有關                 statutory reserve until the cumulative total of the
         部門批准後，法定儲備可用於抵銷                  reserve reaches 50% of the companies registered
         任何累計虧損或增加公司的註冊資                  capital. Subject to approval from the relevant PRC
         本。法定儲備不可用於向中國附屬                  authorities, the statutory reserve may be used to
         公司的權益持有人分派股息。                    offset any accumulated losses or increase the
                                          registered capital of the companies. The statutory
                                          reserve is not available for dividend distribution to
                                          equity holders of the PRC subsidiaries.


   (b)   資本儲備                       (b)   Capital reserve


         資本儲備指(i)本公司權益持有人的注               The capital reserve represents (i) the excess of
         資超出股本的部分；(ii)收購本集團               capital contributions from the equity holders of the
         附屬公司的非控股權益及(iii)於2020            Company over the share capital; (ii) the acquisition
         年轉換為股份有限公司。資本儲備                  of non-controlling interests in the Group’s
         變動詳情載於財務報表的綜合權益                  subsidiaries and (iii) the conversion into a joint
         變動表。                             stock company with limited liability underwent in
                                          2020. Details of the movement in capital reserve
                                          are set out in the consolidated statement of
                                          changes in equity of the financial statements.


   (c)   以股權結算的股份支付儲備               (c)   Equity-settled share-based payment reserve

44. 財務風險管理目標及政策           44. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES
                              AND POLICIES


  本集團主要的金融工具包括以公允價值計         The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise
  量且其變動計入損益的金融資產、現金及         financial assets at fair value through profit or loss,
  銀行結餘及計息銀行借款。本集團擁有直         cash and bank balances and interest-bearing bank
  接源自其運營的多種其他金融資產和負          borrowings. The Group has various other financial
  債，例如貿易應收款項及貿易應付款項。         assets and liabilities such as trade receivables and trade
                             payables, which arise directly from its operations.


  本集團已採納資金管理政策，當中載明購         The Group has adopted a treasury policy which sets out
  買及贖回理財產品的整體原則及詳細審批         overall principles as well as detailed approval processes
  流程。                        of the purchase and redemption of wealth management
                             products.


  本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為外匯風         The main risks arising from the Group’s financial
  險、利率風險、信貸風險及流動性風險。         instruments are foreign currency risk, interest rate risk,
  本公司管理層審查並批准了管理上述各種         credit risk and liquidity risk. The management of the
  風險的政策，有關政策概述於下文。           Company reviews and agrees policies for managing
                             each of these risks and they are summarised below.


  外匯風險                       Foreign currency risk


  本集團存在交易性貨幣風險。該等風險來         The Group has transactional currency exposures. Such
  自運營單位以該單位功能貨幣以外的貨幣         exposures arise from sales or purchases by operating
  進行的買賣。此外，本集團主要因現金及         units in currencies other than the units’ functional
  現金等價物、貿易應收款項及貿易應付款         currencies. In addition, the Group has currency
  項而面臨貨幣風險。                  exposures mainly from its cash and cash equivalent,
                             trade receivables and trade payables.


  本集團會密切監察外匯風險，並在必要時         The Group will monitor the foreign currency exposure
  採取措施以保證外匯風險在可控範圍內。         closely, and will take measures when necessary to make
                             sure the foreign exchange risks are manageable.
